QUALITY DIPS V3 — OPEN (2026-09-18)
Goal: replace V2 double-conservative entry math with a professional evidence-weighted margin-of-safety model while preserving V2/V2.1 unchanged.
Initial implementation commits:
439f3e9 atlas: open V3 evidence-weighted margin-of-safety cycle
2df5a80 atlas: attach V3 entry research beside frozen V2

V3 initial policy:
- robust fair-value anchor requires complete conservative/base/optimistic normalization distribution
- L1/L2/L3/L4 research zones use 15/20/25/30 percent margins of safety from the robust anchor
- ACCUMULATION requires >=15 percent discount plus MEDIUM/HIGH evidence, quality >=75, fundamentals >=70, valuation >=50, intact thesis, and no value-trap gate
- DEEP_VALUE and GENERATIONAL require progressively stronger quality/fundamentals/valuation/drawdown/evidence stacks
- zero qualifying stocks is valid
- no guarantee of undervaluation, bottom, return, or recovery
- V3 remains MANUAL_ONLY until point-in-time DEVELOPMENT/HOLDOUT validation is GREEN

NEXT ENGINEERING GATES — DO NOT SKIP
1. Add focused V3 tests for fail-closed evidence, realistic entry math, state boundaries, and no-lookahead behavior.
2. Render V3 clearly in Quality Dips UI and remove ambiguity between legacy ACCUMULATE, frozen V2, and V3.
3. Add durable V3 L1 transition alerts and frozen entry snapshots.
4. Run DEVELOPMENT then untouched HOLDOUT validation. Do not tune on HOLDOUT.
5. Perps: preserve historical FAILED_NEGATIVE result; improve PAPER/SHADOW only. Manual signals must flow through the same paper execution simulator so missed human clicks are represented in testing.
6. Add explicit paper order lifecycle: CREATED -> PENDING -> FILLED/CANCELLED/EXPIRED/INTERRUPTED. On graceful Atlas shutdown, unfilled paper orders become INTERRUPTED/CANCELLED and must never become phantom fills after restart. Preserve closed fills/trades for clean research; do not delete historical outcomes.
7. Add startup reconciliation for orphaned paper orders after crashes/forced shutdown.
8. Add execution idempotency/dedupe so one signal cannot create duplicate simulated/live orders.
9. Add kill switch, max loss/exposure limits, stale-data rejection, slippage/fee modeling, and broker/exchange acknowledgement reconciliation before any future live-capital gate.
10. Automatic REAL-MONEY trading remains disabled. A future live gate requires separate validation evidence and explicit user-controlled enablement; it must not be enabled merely because PAPER is GREEN.

PERPS HISTORICAL RESULT — STILL IMMUTABLE
Historical experiment remains FAILED_NEGATIVE. Aggregate HOLDOUT 546 trades / -26.7235R / -0.04894R expectancy. Do not relabel, erase, or tune this result. New perps work is a separate PAPER/SHADOW recovery cycle.

THIRD PASS REPAIR — EXPIRY TEST IDENTITY FIX
Latest user rerun after corrected lifecycle test contract:
- focused subset: 11 passed, 1 failed in 1.89s
- remaining failure was test_stale_pending_limit_expires_before_fill
- observed cancellation reason DETACHED_AFTER_RETENTION, meaning the test's pending setup_instance_id did not match the live setup identity
Root cause:
- pending key/setup_instance_id used BTC|a|a
- production _instance_id(setup) correctly generates BTC:LONG|a|a from setup_key + first_seen_at + epoch
- therefore the order was correctly treated as detached, never eligible for age-expiry semantics
Repair:
- production code unchanged
- expiry test now uses the exact production setup_instance_id BTC:LONG|a|a

THIRD PASS REPAIR — FRESH RE-ARM TEST CONTRACT FIX
Latest user rerun:
- focused lifecycle subset: 11 passed, 1 failed in 1.53s
- production cancellation correctly emitted EXPIRED_PENDING_LIMIT
- remaining assertion expected pending_count == 0
Root cause:
- after expiring the old pending limit, the same sync pass is allowed to evaluate the still-valid live setup and arm a fresh replacement resting-L1 order
- therefore pending_count may be 1 even though the stale order was correctly removed
Resolution:
- production code unchanged
- test now asserts the real invariant: EXPIRED_PENDING_LIMIT occurred for the old order; any pending order after sync must be the current runtime pending state, potentially a newly armed replacement
Commit:
0c9e5ad atlas: test expiry without forbidding fresh re-arm

PASS 17 — PID DIAGNOSTIC CAPTURE BUG FOUND AND REPAIRED
Latest user validation:
- focused suite: 26 passed / 1 dependency warning in 12.52s
- Atlas self-start succeeded
- initial health/dashboard/research HTTP 200; command-center and reconciliation timed out during startup
- longevity: 14 probes / 4 failed probes / 6 endpoint failures / max consecutive 1 / recovery observed
- first failure snapshot showed launcher alive and Atlas containers alive
- port_8000_listeners and api_process_tree were unexpectedly empty while api_processes still found both uvicorn command lines

Root cause in diagnostic instrumentation:
- PowerShell $PID is an automatic read-only variable (case-insensitive)
- the new snapshot helpers assigned to $pid, causing their try blocks to fail and silently return empty arrays
- therefore the empty listener/process-tree arrays are invalid diagnostic evidence and MUST NOT be interpreted as port 8000 having no listener

Repair:
- 43fc594 renames local capture variables to $ownerPid and $processId
- 8828f6b adds regression coverage forbidding assignment to reserved $PID
- production runtime behavior is unchanged; diagnostic capture only
- automatic real-money execution remains disabled

2026-10-02 ROADMAP ADDENDUM — PREDICTION MARKETS FIRST, THEN STRATEGY EVIDENCE
================================================================================

PURPOSE
Atlas is now mature enough that software/platform completion and strategy readiness
must be treated as separate gates. A feature-complete trading platform is NOT proof
of profitable edge and is NOT, by itself, authorization for live capital.

CURRENT PRIORITY — FINISH THE PREDICTION MARKETS PAPER LAYER
[ACTIVE] Complete the genuine Prediction PAPER auto-flat validation already in
progress before beginning the strategy-readiness program below.
- Preserve single-market / one-position-max Prediction PAPER safety.
- Preserve real executable orderbook depth for PAPER entry/exit evidence.
- Preserve mandatory pre-event flattening.
- Preserve eligible-candidate Discord notification-only workflow.
- Do not weaken scoring, liquidity, spread, history, stability, edge, or timing
  filters merely to manufacture eligible candidates or favorable PAPER results.
- Do not enable unattended Prediction PAPER opening until a genuine eligible
  position has been observed through its real mandatory auto-flat path and the
  persisted exit evidence is verified.
- Live Prediction execution remains disabled regardless of PAPER auto-flat result.
- live_capital_allowed=false.
- automatic_real_money_execution=false.

[NEXT AFTER PREDICTION AUTO-FLAT VALIDATION]
DAY TRADING + INVESTMENT STRATEGY EVIDENCE / LIVE-READINESS PROGRAM

PRINCIPLE
The next phase is not "add features until Atlas feels finished." It is:
measure -> explain -> improve -> freeze -> validate on unseen evidence -> repeat.
Poor/subpar PAPER performance is evidence to investigate, not a reason to loosen
risk controls, overfit historical data, increase trade frequency, or rush live.

PHASE A — FREEZE A COMPARABLE DAY-TRADING BASELINE
[PLANNED]
- Freeze the current core strategy/policy/execution versions for a bounded evidence
  window. Continue fixing software/data-integrity defects, but do not silently tune
  strategy parameters between sessions.
- Persist exact strategy, policy, execution-model and relevant configuration
  versions with every candidate/trade/outcome.
- Treat materially changed strategy logic as a new cohort/version.
- Preserve negative evidence. Never rewrite historical PAPER results after tuning.
- Use macro/high-volatility sessions as labeled regimes, not one-off excuses or
  opportunities for ad-hoc parameter changes.

PHASE B — AUTOMATIC LOSS / TRADE ATTRIBUTION
[PLANNED]
For every closed PAPER trade, preserve enough evidence to distinguish at least:
- direction/signal failure
- entry too early / entry too late
- signal decay before execution
- spread/slippage/liquidity degradation
- stop/exit behavior
- target/repricing assumption failure
- time-of-day effects
- market-regime mismatch
- macro/event interference
- execution-model/data-quality issue
- genuinely negative strategy edge / unresolved cause
Attribution must be evidence-backed; UNKNOWN remains valid when causality cannot
be established from captured data.

PHASE C — REGIME-AWARE PERFORMANCE ANALYSIS
[PLANNED]
Measure strategy cohorts independently across:
- trend vs chop/range
- high vs normal/low volatility
- opening period / morning / midday / afternoon
- macro-event vs ordinary sessions
- broad risk-on / risk-off where supported by captured evidence
- liquidity/spread conditions
Do not assume one setup should trade identically in every regime.
Do not introduce regime filters unless they are supported by prospective or
properly separated validation evidence.

PHASE D — STRATEGY-LEVEL SCORECARDS
[PLANNED]
Per strategy/version/regime report:
- sample size and evidence dates
- win/loss/scratch counts and win rate
- average/median win and loss
- expectancy
- profit factor where mathematically defined
- cumulative PAPER R / PnL under the recorded execution model
- maximum drawdown
- MAE/MFE where captured
- spread, slippage and fees
- holding time and time-of-day
- unresolved/invalid evidence counts
No strategy is promoted merely for a high win rate. Economic expectancy and
drawdown matter, and insufficient sample sizes remain INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE.

PHASE E — RETIRE OR CHALLENGE NEGATIVE-EDGE BEHAVIOR
[PLANNED]
- Strategies with sufficiently mature, consistently negative out-of-sample evidence
  become candidates for disablement/replacement rather than endless parameter
  optimization.
- Challengers must be versioned and evaluated without rewriting champion history.
- Do not cherry-pick only favorable regimes or delete losing observations.
- No automatic strategy promotion without predefined evidence criteria and an
  untouched validation segment.

PHASE F — WALK-FORWARD / OUT-OF-SAMPLE VALIDATION
[PLANNED]
- Development/tuning evidence and validation evidence must be separated.
- Freeze each candidate policy before evaluating the next unseen segment.
- Record every freeze boundary, policy hash/version, rationale and outcome.
- Require performance to survive multiple unseen periods/regimes rather than one
  favorable day/week.
- Follow with fresh live-market PAPER/shadow validation using the same frozen
  policy and execution assumptions.
- No hindsight reclassification of losing validation periods.

PHASE G — INVESTMENT PROGRAM PROSPECTIVE SCORECARD
[PLANNED / BUILDS ON EXISTING PROSPECTIVE PIPELINE]
Investment research is evaluated separately from intraday trading.

PERMANENT INVESTMENT SCOPE — LONG ONLY
- The Atlas Investment / Quality Dips program is long-only.
- Long-duration investment shorting is permanently out of scope.
- Do not add or revisit Quality Extremes, ATH-exhaustion short strategies, direct
  borrowed-share investment shorts, inverse-ETF bearish investment strategies,
  long-duration bearish hedging strategies, or leveraged bearish investment trades.
- Do not add margin/borrow-based investment execution as a future Atlas milestone.
- HOLD / WAIT / BUY_NOTHING_TODAY is the bearish or insufficient-edge response for
  the Investment program; Atlas is not required to monetize every downward thesis.
- This permanent exclusion does not rewrite preserved historical intraday PAPER
  execution evidence; it governs the Investment / long-horizon roadmap and future work.
- Reintroducing investment shorting is not a deferred task and is not part of the
  continuous refinement loop.

For each frozen investment observation/call:
- preserve only information available at observation time
- evaluate forward horizons such as 1d / 5d / 20d / 60d when mature
- compare against an appropriate recorded benchmark/reference series
- retain drawdown, opportunity-cost and unresolved outcomes
- retain calibration by confidence/score bucket where sample size supports it
- preserve valuation/actionability provenance and policy/model version
- never backfill hindsight facts into the original decision
Investment readiness must be based on mature prospective evidence, not the fact
that the Investment UI/research pipeline is feature-complete.

PHASE H — LIVE-READINESS GATE
[BLOCKED UNTIL POSITIVE EVIDENCE]
A small live-capital pilot may be CONSIDERED only after all applicable evidence
gates are satisfied. No fixed profitability threshold is invented here merely to
unlock live trading. The readiness review must include:
- stable/versioned strategy definition
- adequate prospective/out-of-sample sample size
- positive economic expectancy after recorded costs under the tested model
- understood drawdown/loss distribution
- acceptable execution quality/slippage/liquidity behavior
- evidence across relevant regimes rather than a single favorable period
- no unresolved material data-integrity/runtime defects
- successful fresh PAPER/shadow validation after the final strategy freeze
- explicit operator review and separate authorization
Until then:
- Day Trading remains PAPER/shadow where automatic execution is concerned.
- Investment remains research/manual decision support.
- Prediction Markets remain PAPER research unless separately authorized later.
- live_capital_allowed=false.
- automatic_real_money_execution=false.

PHASE I — IF A FUTURE SMALL LIVE PILOT IS EXPLICITLY AUTHORIZED
[NOT AUTHORIZED / FUTURE ONLY]
- Start deliberately small and bounded.
- Hard daily/session/position loss limits and kill switch.
- No automatic scaling from early wins.
- Compare live fills/slippage/behavior against the validated PAPER assumptions.
- Any material divergence returns the strategy to PAPER/shadow investigation.
- Scaling requires a new evidence review; it is never implied by software uptime.

ORDER OF OPERATIONS
1. Finish current Prediction Markets genuine PAPER auto-flat validation.
2. Close any remaining Prediction PAPER integrity/observability defects found by
   that real validation.
3. Freeze the Day Trading baseline and begin comparable forward evidence.
4. Add automatic trade/loss attribution and regime labeling/reporting.
5. Produce strategy/version/regime scorecards.
6. Retire or challenge negative-edge behavior using preserved evidence.
7. Run walk-forward/out-of-sample validation and fresh frozen PAPER confirmation.
8. Continue maturing the Investment prospective 1d/5d/20d/60d evidence and
   benchmark/opportunity-cost/calibration reporting.
9. Perform a formal read-only live-readiness review.
10. Only after positive evidence and explicit authorization consider a bounded
    small live-capital pilot. Real-money automation is not implied or pre-approved.

STATUS AT ADDENDUM
- Prediction Markets: active priority; genuine PAPER auto-flat validation pending.
- Day Trading platform: operationally mature, strategy edge still requires further
  evidence/improvement; historical negative PAPER evidence remains preserved.
- Investment research platform: substantial prospective foundation exists, but
  mature forward evidence remains the limiting factor.
- The roadmap therefore optimizes next for PROVABLE EDGE AND EXECUTION QUALITY,
  not additional feature count and not premature live-capital activation.

CONTINUOUS ATLAS REFINEMENT MODE — AFTER REMAINING BUILD PHASES
SCOPE FREEZE:
- Do not add new strategy families or speculative feature branches by default.
- New work must address a demonstrated need in safety, data integrity, execution
  realism, reliability, observability, evidence quality, or measured strategy weakness.
- Prefer long-duration PAPER/shadow evidence and refinement of existing systems over
  expanding Atlas simply because another trade idea is possible.
- Prediction Markets, Day Trading, and Investment/Quality Dips are the intended
  strategy/research scope.
- Investment/Quality Dips remains LONG ONLY permanently; continuous refinement must
  not generate, propose, or schedule long-horizon short/hedge/inverse strategies.

Once the planned engineering/research surfaces above are implemented and verified,
Atlas transitions from feature expansion to a continuing evidence loop across:
Prediction Markets, Day Trading, and Investment/Quality Dips.
For each strategy family:
collect prospective PAPER/shadow evidence -> attribute wins/losses -> analyze
regimes/execution/costs -> propose a versioned challenger -> freeze -> validate on
unseen evidence -> retain/reject based on evidence -> repeat.
This loop may continue indefinitely while edge is uncertain or improving.
Software completion never converts PAPER to live automatically. Each live-capital
step requires a separate evidence review, bounded risk design, explicit operator
authorization, and continued ability to return to PAPER/shadow on degradation.
Real-money automation remains NOT AUTHORIZED by this roadmap addendum.

2026-10-04 ROADMAP ADDENDUM — CONTROLLED STRATEGY RESEARCH & PREDICTION EXPANSION
================================================================================

PURPOSE
Capture the useful research concepts identified during external strategy brainstorming
without treating videos, screenshots, promotional claims, or creator P&L as proof of
edge. Atlas must independently reproduce every useful hypothesis with realistic,
timestamped PAPER/shadow evidence before it can influence live-capital readiness.

GOVERNING PRINCIPLE
BRAINSTORM AGGRESSIVELY; CHANGE PRODUCTION CONSERVATIVELY.
Idea -> mechanism -> risks -> frozen hypothesis -> PAPER/shadow -> attribution ->
regime/cost analysis -> versioned challenger -> unseen validation -> retain/reject.
External claims are hypotheses only. Atlas evidence outranks promotional evidence.

CURRENT RUNTIME PROTECTION
[ACTIVE / DO NOT DISRUPT]
- Finish and verify the currently running genuine Prediction PAPER auto-flat lifecycle
  before pulling/restarting Atlas for this addendum.
- This roadmap update is documentation/research scope only and must not alter the
  current runtime, thresholds, PAPER position, auto-flat behavior, or safety flags.
- After the auto-flat checkpoint: verify exactly-once legitimate close, executable
  bid depth/VWAP, fees, pre-event timing, journal durability, and no deadline violation
  or fabricated fill before syncing/restarting.
- live_capital_allowed=false.
- automatic_real_money_execution=false.

PREDICTION RESEARCH FAMILY 1 — EXISTING EARLY-ENTRY STRATEGY
[PRESERVE / DO NOT CONTAMINATE]
PRE_EVENT_RECENT_RECLAIM_V1 remains an independent cohort.
- Preserve the user's original early-buy / pre-event repricing concept.
- Single market only; max one applicable Prediction PAPER position under current policy.
- Real executable orderbook evidence only.
- Mandatory pre-event flattening.
- No hold-to-settlement behavior where the strategy contract requires pre-event exit.
- Do not merge results with future 15-minute or arbitrage cohorts.

PREDICTION RESEARCH FAMILY 2 — 15-MINUTE CRYPTO + METALS/COMMODITIES
[PLANNED / PAPER ONLY]
Create a separately versioned FIFTEEN_MINUTE_MARKETS_V1 research cohort after the
current Prediction lifecycle gate is complete.
Research goals:
- Discover supported rolling 15-minute crypto and metals/commodity markets.
- Preserve contract/reference/settlement metadata rather than assuming spot equivalence.
- Record underlying reference movement, strike/target, time remaining, volatility,
  momentum, Kalshi YES/NO bid/ask, spread, depth, executable size, and timestamps.
- Model PAPER entry/exit only from executable depth; never midpoint/chart-touch fills.
- Record consumed levels, VWAP, fees, slippage, MFE, MAE, exit, resolution, and
  unresolved/data-quality states.
- Analyze performance by asset family separately; do not assume crypto and metals share
  one probability model.
- Analyze entry timing/time-to-expiry buckets and market microstructure.
- Compare implied prediction probability with an independently estimated probability
  only when the underlying/reference mapping is valid and point-in-time.
- Require net edge after fees, spread, slippage, uncertainty, and execution constraints.
- Build a meaningful forward PAPER sample before any live-readiness consideration.

PREDICTION RESEARCH FAMILY 3 — CROSS-VENUE ARBITRAGE
[PLANNED / RESEARCH + PAPER/SHADOW ONLY]
Create CROSS_VENUE_ARB_V1 only for venues/products the operator is permitted to use.
Do not design Atlas to bypass geographic, platform, account, or product restrictions.
A candidate is not an arbitrage merely because titles look similar.
Require a contract-equivalence gate covering:
- same economic event/outcome
- compatible resolution criteria and edge cases
- compatible cutoff/observation times
- compatible settlement/reference source and definitions
- compatible cancellation/voiding treatment where relevant
- sufficiently synchronized executable orderbook timestamps
For a verified equivalent pair calculate:
guaranteed payout - executable YES leg - executable NO leg - fees - slippage -
latency/legging reserve = conservative net arb.
Reject when either leg lacks sufficient executable depth or equivalence is uncertain.
Model one-leg fill/second-leg movement explicitly; never label a trade risk-free.
Measure capital-hours locked, realized convergence time, early-exit vs settlement
economics, fill failure, stale quote rate, and opportunity persistence.
No automatic real-money cross-venue execution is authorized.

PERMANENT PREDICTION CONSTRAINT — NO COMBOS EVER
[PERMANENT]
- No parlays.
- No combo/multivariate products.
- No correlated-outcome stacking packaged as one trade.
- No strategy may silently reintroduce combos under another name.
- Strategy research remains independently attributable by market and cohort.

PREDICTION WALLET INTELLIGENCE
[PLANNED / FEATURE + RESEARCH LAYER, NOT BLIND COPY TRADING]
Build wallet intelligence only where public/permitted data supports it.
Rank/characterize wallets using evidence such as:
- ROI/economic performance
- consistency and concentration of returns
- sample size and evidence window
- category-specific skill (crypto, macro, sports, politics, etc.)
- liquidity/copyability
- typical entry timing
- price movement between source-wallet entry and Atlas-observable entry
- spread/depth at Atlas-observable time
- one-hit-wonder / concentrated-profit risk
For each observed wallet signal classify COPY_CANDIDATE / WATCH / SKIP for PAPER
research, with explicit reasons and evidence. Do not treat a profitable wallet as
universally skilled.
Required falsification baseline:
- compare filtered wallet intelligence against blind copying of the same eligible
  wallet universe under identical PAPER execution assumptions.
- track missed winners and avoided losers as well as copied outcomes.
- if the intelligence layer cannot beat the baseline robustly out of sample, it adds
  no demonstrated edge and must not be promoted.
Wallet activity may become corroborating evidence for an Atlas strategy but must not
override execution, liquidity, safety, contract-validity, or evidence gates.

ATLAS STRATEGY RESEARCHER — CONTROLLED SELF-IMPROVEMENT
[PLANNED / READ-ONLY PROPOSAL AUTHORITY FIRST]
Build a supervisory research loop on top of the existing journals, attribution,
regime diagnostics, scorecards, prospective evidence, and versioned cohorts.
Loop:
1. observe frozen strategy outcomes
2. detect statistically/economically meaningful weakness
3. distinguish execution/data defect from possible strategy defect
4. form an evidence-backed hypothesis
5. propose ONE controlled change where feasible
6. create a new challenger version/cohort
7. keep incumbent history immutable
8. run PAPER/shadow development evidence
9. freeze challenger before unseen validation
10. evaluate on unseen/forward evidence
11. retain/reject; never rewrite the losing history
12. repeat only when evidence justifies another hypothesis
The Researcher must never optimize directly on untouched HOLDOUT evidence.
UNKNOWN/INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE are valid conclusions.
No automatic promotion to live capital.

ATLAS STRATEGY SUPERVISOR
[PLANNED / OBSERVABILITY + GOVERNANCE]
Add a supervisor that asks whether deterioration is likely:
- normal variance / insufficient sample
- regime-specific weakness
- execution/slippage/liquidity degradation
- data/provider defect
- strategy/policy degradation
- unresolved
Monitor:
- rolling expectancy and profit factor where meaningful
- drawdown and loss clustering
- MFE/MAE and exit capture
- costs/slippage/spread
- fill/rejection/timeout/reconciliation quality
- regime distribution
- stale/missing data
- strategy/version drift
- sample maturity
The supervisor may flag, pause PAPER research, or propose a challenger according to
future explicit policy, but it must not silently rewrite an active strategy.

IMMUTABLE RISK / GOVERNANCE BOUNDARY
[PERMANENT]
No research agent, strategy supervisor, LLM, optimizer, or automated rule updater may:
- enable live trading
- increase live risk/exposure limits
- disable kill switches
- remove stale-data/liquidity/execution gates
- introduce combos/parlays
- alter the permanent long-only Investment scope
- erase/relabel negative historical evidence
- promote a strategy solely from in-sample performance
- silently change an incumbent strategy while its evidence window is active
Risk constraints and live authorization sit outside strategy-learning authority.

CHANGE CONTROL / ANTI-OVERFITTING
[REQUIRED]
- Prefer one-variable challenger changes where scientifically meaningful.
- Every change requires version, timestamp, rationale, evidence reference, and diff.
- Preserve champion and challenger results independently.
- Do not tune after every loss.
- Require minimum evidence maturity before diagnosing edge failure.
- Correct software/data defects without pretending they are strategy improvements.
- Multiple-comparison/data-mining risk must be acknowledged when many challengers are
  tried; repeated experimentation does not make a lucky winner proven.
- Fresh forward PAPER/shadow evidence is required after the final freeze.

DECISION JOURNAL EXPANSION
[PLANNED]
For every candidate decision, including rejected/skipped opportunities where feasible,
preserve:
- strategy/version and policy hash
- point-in-time features and provenance
- decision: ENTER/COPY_CANDIDATE/WATCH/SKIP/REJECT as applicable
- explicit reason codes
- executable market state
- regime/context
- subsequent outcome path
- whether a skip avoided a loser or missed a winner
- attribution after maturity
This journal supports learning from both actions and non-actions without fabricating
counterfactual fills.

EVIDENCE-BASED PROMOTION LADDER
[PLANNED]
RESEARCH_IDEA
-> PAPER_DEVELOPMENT
-> FROZEN_CHALLENGER
-> UNSEEN_VALIDATION
-> FRESH_FORWARD_PAPER/SHADOW
-> READ_ONLY_LIVE_READINESS_REVIEW
-> only with separate explicit operator authorization: BOUNDED_LIVE_PILOT
No stage is automatically promoted by uptime, AI confidence, a YouTube claim, a
backtest, one profitable day, or a visually impressive dashboard.

DEFI / FLASH-LOAN ARBITRAGE
[DEFERRED / NOT CURRENT ATLAS SCOPE]
Same-chain DEX/flash-loan arbitrage is technically legitimate research but introduces
a separate stack: smart contracts, DEX routing, simulation, MEV competition, private
transaction infrastructure, chain-specific failure modes, and smart-contract risk.
Do not add it to the current Atlas implementation roadmap unless future evidence and
operator intent justify opening a separate explicitly scoped cycle/project.
Never import or fund unverified third-party "AI arbitrage bot" contracts.

EXTERNAL-IDEA INTAKE POLICY
[PERMANENT]
Videos, posts, screenshots, creator dashboards, affiliate tools, and claimed profits
may generate research hypotheses only.
For each external idea Atlas development should ask:
- What is the actual mechanism?
- What must be true for the edge to exist?
- What execution/cost/latency risks are omitted?
- Can it be tested with point-in-time data?
- What falsification baseline should beat it?
- Can Atlas reproduce it prospectively without trusting the creator's P&L?
Reject ideas that cannot be tested cleanly or that require bypassing access rules,
unsafe custody, unverifiable smart contracts, or fabricated execution assumptions.

UPDATED ORDER AFTER CURRENT AUTO-FLAT CHECKPOINT
1. Verify current Prediction genuine auto-flat evidence before restart/sync.
2. Sync pending Atlas commits; restart cleanly; validate attribution/scorecards and
   ACTIONABLE ONLY display filter without changing strategy thresholds.
3. Continue Day Trading baseline/attribution/regime/scorecard evidence program.
4. Add 15-minute crypto + metals/commodity Prediction PAPER collection as an
   independent cohort.
5. Add contract-equivalence and cross-venue arbitrage research infrastructure where
   permitted data/venues support it.
6. Add Prediction wallet-intelligence research with blind-copy baseline.
7. Add controlled Strategy Researcher/Supervisor proposal and governance surfaces.
8. Run versioned challengers through development -> freeze -> unseen -> fresh forward
   PAPER/shadow validation.
9. Continue maturing long-only Investment prospective evidence independently.
10. Formal read-only live-readiness review only after positive mature evidence.
11. Any real-money pilot remains separate, deliberately small, bounded, kill-switched,
    explicitly authorized, and reversible to PAPER/shadow.

SUCCESS DEFINITION
The goal is not for Atlas to change itself frequently. The goal is for Atlas to become
progressively harder to fool: by hype, overfitting, stale data, unrealistic fills,
lucky wallets, favorable regimes, hidden costs, or its own prior assumptions.

================================================================================
2026-10-04 ROADMAP ADDENDUM — REVERSAL / SELECTIVITY RESEARCH LESSONS
================================================================================

SOURCE HANDLING
These items were extracted from an external trading-education video and are hypotheses
or research-design lessons, NOT validated Atlas edge. Creator profit/win-rate/account
claims are not imported as evidence. Atlas must reproduce any useful mechanism with
its own point-in-time PAPER/shadow data.

HIGH-VALUE PRINCIPLE — SELECTIVITY OVER TRADE FREQUENCY
[ADOPT AS RESEARCH/GOVERNANCE PRINCIPLE]
- Atlas must never interpret "day trading" as a requirement to produce a trade daily.
- Zero-trade sessions are valid when the frozen edge is absent.
- Measure opportunity quality and expectancy rather than rewarding raw trade count.
- Do not loosen filters to satisfy activity targets.
- Scorecards should expose trade frequency alongside expectancy so increased activity
  cannot masquerade as strategy improvement.

CHALLENGER FAMILY — OVEREXTENSION REVERSAL
[PLANNED / PAPER-SHADOW RESEARCH ONLY]
Create a versioned OVEREXTENSION_REVERSAL_V1 challenger only after current evidence
priorities permit it; do not silently alter the incumbent perps strategy.
Test the hypothesis that unusually extended directional moves may mean-revert when
multiple independent conditions align.
Candidate point-in-time features:
- normalized displacement/extension from recent range or channel
- acceleration outside an established directional channel
- raw volume and relative-volume expansion
- distance to higher-timeframe support/resistance / prior swing extremes
- multi-timeframe trend/range context
- spread, liquidity, volatility and executable depth
- time-of-day/session bucket
- scheduled macro/event proximity
- reversal confirmation available before entry
Fair-value-gap concepts may be encoded as testable price-imbalance features, but the
name/pattern itself is not presumed predictive.

MULTI-TIMEFRAME CONTEXT
[ADD TO REGIME/FEATURE RESEARCH]
Where data permits, preserve higher-timeframe context alongside the execution
timeframe. Test whether setup performance changes when an intraday signal occurs:
- at/near a major higher-timeframe extreme
- in the middle of a range
- with/against the higher-timeframe trend
- during directional, choppy, or volatile regimes
Do not hard-code the video's discretionary chart interpretation as truth. Quantify it
and require prospective/OOS evidence.

RAW-MARKET-DATA VS DERIVED-INDICATOR AUDIT
[PLANNED]
For each challenger, distinguish raw observations (price, volume, spread, depth,
timestamps) from derived indicators. Derived indicators are allowed, but Atlas should
test whether they add incremental out-of-sample information beyond their underlying
price/volume inputs. Remove redundant complexity when it adds no demonstrated edge.

TIME-OF-DAY / EVENT CONDITIONING
[EXPAND EXISTING REGIME ANALYSIS]
- Preserve entry/decision timestamps and session buckets.
- Evaluate setup expectancy by opening period, morning, midday, afternoon and other
  statistically supported buckets rather than assuming a universal "magic time."
- Join scheduled macro/event context point-in-time where reliable evidence exists.
- Never blindly trade a clock time or infer causality from a few examples.
- Macro/event filters require independent prospective validation.

SETUP QUALITY GRADING
[PLANNED / MUST BE CALIBRATED]
Research an evidence-based setup-quality score/tier (for example A/B/C internally)
built from frozen, measurable features. Do NOT copy subjective grades or risk sizes
from a creator.
Required validation:
- reliability/calibration: do higher grades actually have better forward expectancy?
- sample size per grade
- expectancy/profit factor/drawdown/MFE/MAE after costs
- regime and time-of-day stability
- no circular grading using future outcome
Until calibrated, setup grade is descriptive only and must not increase live risk.

R-MULTIPLE / PAYOFF DISCIPLINE
[ADOPT / EXTEND SCORECARDS]
Continue using R-normalized outcomes so strategy quality is not confused with account
dollars. Explicitly report:
- win rate
- average/median winner and loser in R
- expectancy in R
- payoff ratio
- profit factor
- max drawdown in R
A low win-rate strategy can be valid only if forward net expectancy after realistic
costs is positive; high win rate alone is never sufficient.

EXIT / PROFIT-CAPTURE CHALLENGERS
[PLANNED / PAPER RESEARCH]
Use existing MFE/MAE and attribution evidence to test exit-management alternatives
without rewriting entries:
- fixed target/stop baseline
- break-even transition rules
- partial scale-out variants
- trailing-stop variants
- runner/remainder variants
- time-based exits
Every exit policy must be a separately versioned execution-policy cohort. Compare
net expectancy, drawdown, captured-MFE efficiency, costs and tail outcomes. Do not
assume scaling out is superior merely because it feels safer.

PRE-TRADE PLAN / POST-TRADE ATTRIBUTION
[EXPAND DECISION JOURNAL]
Before PAPER entry, freeze:
- setup thesis and version
- invalidation/stop logic
- initial target / expected reward-to-risk
- optional stretch objective if defined by policy
- setup-quality features/grade
- regime/time/event context
After close, compare plan vs execution and outcome:
- planned vs actual entry
- planned vs actual exit
- MFE/MAE
- profit capture/giveback
- execution costs
- whether management followed the frozen policy
This separates strategy weakness from discretionary/execution drift.

SKIPPED-OPPORTUNITY EVIDENCE
[REINFORCE]
Preserve qualifying setups Atlas deliberately skips/rejects and mature their forward
paths without fabricating fills. Measure missed winners, avoided losers and rejected
setup expectancy. This is necessary to determine whether selectivity improves edge
rather than merely reducing trade count.

ANTI-LESSON — DO NOT IMPORT THESE CLAIMS AS POLICY
[PERMANENT CAUTION]
Do not adopt from external creator anecdotes:
- fixed morning-only windows without Atlas evidence
- claimed reversal times as universal signals
- creator A+/A/B sizing percentages
- exponential account-growth assumptions
- account-dollar goals
- the claim that one indicator/pattern guarantees reversal
- creator-reported P&L or win rate as validation
- larger risk simply because a setup is labeled high quality
Any future risk scaling requires Atlas-specific calibration, adequate forward/OOS
evidence, hard caps, and the separate live-risk governance gate.

RELATION TO CONTROLLED SELF-IMPROVEMENT
The Strategy Researcher may use these measurements to generate hypotheses such as
"losses cluster in mid-range/choppy conditions" or "positive MFE is repeatedly given
back by the current exit policy." It may propose a versioned challenger. It may NOT
retroactively change the incumbent, tune on untouched HOLDOUT, or increase live risk.

STATUS
[ROADMAP ONLY] No runtime strategy, thresholds, risk, PAPER position, Prediction
auto-flat process, or live-capital permission changed by this addendum.
================================================================================

2026-10-05 PREDICTION AUTO-FLAT OBSERVATION — SAFE FAILURE / LIQUIDITY HARDENING
================================================================================
OBSERVED EVIDENCE
- Genuine PAPER trade 21aa114b-10d reached the mandatory flatten window.
- Auto-flat attempted against the real public orderbook and found no executable bid
  depth for the required exit.
- Atlas recorded AUTO_FLAT_BLOCKED_NO_DEPTH / NO_EXECUTABLE_DEPTH.
- Atlas did NOT manufacture a close, settlement fill, midpoint fill, or hidden depth.
- The trade remained open in the PAPER journal and unattended PAPER opening remained
  blocked by AUTO_FLAT_SAFETY_BLOCKED + AUTO_FLAT_LIVE_VALIDATION_REQUIRED.
- Classification: AUTO-FLAT VALIDATION FAILED SAFELY.
- Negative evidence is permanent and must not be rewritten as a pass.

ENGINEERING RESPONSE
[IMPLEMENTED ON GITHUB; REQUIRES CONTROLLED LOCAL VALIDATION]
- Candidate eligibility now requires full executable SELL depth for the requested
  quantity at entry time, in addition to executable BUY depth.
- New rejection evidence distinguishes no exit depth vs insufficient exit depth.
- Candidate evidence stores an exit_liquidity_at_entry snapshot.
- Mandatory auto-flat attempts now begin 10 minutes before the strategy's existing
  flat deadline rather than only five seconds before it.
- The worker continues retrying on its existing five-second cadence; lack of depth
  remains a hard block and never creates a fake close.
- Tests were added for the exit-liquidity entry gate and early auto-flat retry window.
- These GitHub tests are not considered passed until run in a controlled local/CI
  environment.

SAFETY / PROMOTION GATE
- Do NOT remove AUTO_FLAT_LIVE_VALIDATION_REQUIRED from this failed observation.
- Do NOT enable unattended Prediction PAPER opening yet.
- Require a future genuine PAPER lifecycle to demonstrate an executable, exactly-once,
  pre-deadline auto-flat after these hardening changes.
- Current changes reduce entry/exit-liquidity risk; they do not prove future liquidity.
- Live execution and automatic real-money execution remain false.

================================================================================
PREDICTION EXPIRED-UNCLOSED TERMINAL ACCOUNTING — 2026-10-05
================================================================================

Observed post-restart evidence showed PAPER trade 21aa114b-10d remained journal-open
after its event occurrence because mandatory auto-flat had no executable bid depth.
Atlas correctly refused to fabricate a close and later reported a deadline violation.

Hardening added:
- A trade that reaches event start without an executable exit is now append-only
  terminal state EXPIRED_UNCLOSED, not a synthetic close.
- No exit price, exit notional, exit fee, fill levels, gross/net PnL, ROI, settlement
  result, or win/loss is manufactured for this state.
- The most recent pre-event auto-flat blocker is preserved. No/insufficient depth
  maps to terminal reason EXIT_FAILED_NO_LIQUIDITY; other unresolved failures map
  to EXIT_FAILED_BEFORE_EVENT_START.
- EXPIRED_UNCLOSED no longer counts as an active/open position and does not count
  as a closed trade. It is reported separately in Prediction PAPER summary.
- Reconciliation is idempotent: repeated worker checks do not append duplicate
  terminal rows.
- A durable EXPIRED_UNCLOSED state keeps AUTO_FLAT_SAFETY_BLOCKED active after
  restart/reconciliation.
- AUTO_FLAT_LIVE_VALIDATION_REQUIRED remains in force. Unattended Prediction PAPER
  opening remains disabled. Live execution/capital remain disabled.
- No combos/parlays/multivariate/stacking policy was changed.
- Tests were added/updated for terminal accounting, no fake PnL/fill, idempotence,
  liquidity-failure attribution, and durable safety blocking.

Validation status:
- PASSED controlled local validation on 2026-10-05 after sync to commit 3678e56.
- Focused Prediction automation regression: 14 passed, 0 failed.
- Full Atlas regression: 1153 passed, 0 failed; only the two previously known
  dependency deprecation warnings remained.
- Genuine journal trade 21aa114b-10d reconciled at runtime to EXPIRED_UNCLOSED with
  terminal reason EXIT_FAILED_NO_LIQUIDITY.
- Runtime reconciliation preserved the prior AUTO_FLAT_BLOCKED_NO_DEPTH /
  NO_EXECUTABLE_DEPTH evidence and manufactured no exit price, exit notional, fee,
  fill, gross/net PnL, ROI, settlement result, or closed-trade outcome.
- Runtime auto-flat state reported TERMINAL_UNCLOSED, blocked=true and
  deadline_violation=true.
- Scanner remained healthy during acceptance: 400 metadata markets seen, 40 selected,
  7 fully evaluated, 0 scanner errors and 0 timeouts in the captured cycle.
- AUTO_FLAT_SAFETY_BLOCKED and AUTO_FLAT_LIVE_VALIDATION_REQUIRED remain active.
  Unattended Prediction PAPER opening remains disabled; live execution/capital and
  automatic real-money execution remain false.
- Classification: EXPIRED-UNCLOSED LIFECYCLE HARDENING PASSED.
- This pass validates safe accounting/reconciliation of a failed exit. It does NOT
  satisfy the separate future requirement for a genuine successful exactly-once
  pre-deadline auto-flat lifecycle.

===============================================================================
2026-10-05 UNIVERSAL PAPER PORTFOLIO / INVESTMENT ARCHIVE EXPANSION
===============================================================================
Operator goal:
- Evolve Atlas so every supported strategy family can be evaluated through a common,
  durable PAPER execution/evidence lifecycle before any future live-capital review.
- Make Archive -> Investment History materially more useful for Quality Dips and
  future investment strategies rather than treating it as a passive history screen.
- Preserve the separation between PAPER evidence and real brokerage execution.

Observed UI reference supplied 2026-10-05:
- Archive currently provides PAPER TRADES, INVESTMENT HISTORY, RESEARCH ARCHIVE,
  SNAPSHOTS, and EXPORT.
- Existing PAPER archive already exposes aggregate performance, strategy/setup
  breakdowns, direction, trade details, MFE/MAE, holding time, and an equity curve.
- This is the design baseline to extend rather than replace.

TARGET ARCHITECTURE — UNIVERSAL PAPER EXECUTION
[PLANNED]
Create a shared PAPER portfolio/execution contract capable of serving:
- Day Trading / perps
- Quality Dips / long-only investments
- Prediction Markets
- future strategy families only after their own explicit policy and safety contract

Each family keeps its own strategy/scoring rules. The common layer owns durable
simulation concerns: candidate -> intended order -> executable PAPER fill -> open
position -> management -> exit/terminal state -> immutable archive evidence.
No strategy becomes live merely because it is supported by the common PAPER layer.

QUALITY DIPS PAPER PORTFOLIO
[PLANNED / LONG ONLY]
Add a dedicated versioned Quality Dips PAPER policy that mirrors qualifying frozen
investment decisions into simulated positions without placing Robinhood/broker orders.
Requirements:
- Preserve the current long-only Investment boundary permanently.
- Record policy/version, symbol, point-in-time thesis/evidence, V3 state, L1-L4 level,
  intended quantity/notional policy, observed market price, simulated fill model,
  timestamp, cost basis, position size, and provenance.
- Track partial staged accumulation as explicit PAPER lots rather than silently
  rewriting average cost.
- Never average down merely because price fell; each simulated addition must satisfy
  the frozen Quality Dips policy at that point in time.
- Preserve thesis changes, THESIS_BROKEN, stale evidence, HOLD/WAIT/BUY_NOTHING_TODAY,
  and skipped opportunities.
- Model fees/slippage where applicable; never assume a fill from a chart touch alone.
- Define versioned PAPER exit/management rules before scoring performance. Do not
  invent exits retrospectively from favorable future prices.
- Shutdown/restart must preserve open PAPER investment positions and journal state.

INVESTMENT ARCHIVE V2
[PLANNED]
Upgrade Archive -> Investment History into an evidence dashboard with:
- open vs closed PAPER investment positions
- realized and unrealized PAPER P&L clearly separated
- cumulative return/equity curve and drawdown
- per-symbol and per-policy/version attribution
- L1/L2/L3/L4 lot history and weighted cost basis
- thesis state and thesis-change timeline
- holding duration
- MFE/MAE and capture/giveback after positions close
- skipped/rejected opportunity outcomes without fabricated fills
- benchmark comparison where point-in-time benchmark data is available
- regime/time-period breakdown
- execution/fill provenance and data freshness
- filters for strategy version, symbol, state, date, and evidence maturity
- explicit PAPER ONLY / NO BROKER ORDER labeling

UNIVERSAL ARCHIVE / CROSS-STRATEGY SCORECARD
[PLANNED]
Build a common read-only portfolio research surface that can compare strategy families
without mixing incompatible units. Report dollars/percent/R/contract metrics only where
appropriate and preserve family-specific statistics. Include:
- sample size and maturity
- net expectancy after modeled costs
- win/loss or resolved outcome statistics where meaningful
- drawdown
- exposure and concurrency
- holding time
- execution quality
- regime attribution
- strategy/version
- non-performance terminal states
- untouched forward evidence window
Do not combine Day Trading, Investment, and Prediction results into a misleading single
win rate.

PAPER-TO-LIVE PROMOTION CONTRACT
[REQUIRED]
Universal PAPER support is evidence infrastructure, not live authorization.
A future strategy may approach a bounded live pilot only after:
1. implementation/regression safety is GREEN;
2. realistic PAPER execution semantics are validated;
3. strategy policy/version is frozen;
4. unseen and fresh forward evidence is positive after costs;
5. drawdown/tail behavior and regime dependence are acceptable;
6. reconciliation, stale-data, exposure, loss-stop and kill-switch controls pass;
7. a read-only live-readiness review passes;
8. the operator separately and explicitly authorizes a deliberately small live pilot.
No automatic transition from PAPER to live is permitted.

EARNINGS / OPTIMIZATION PRINCIPLE
[PERMANENT]
Atlas optimizes for demonstrated risk-adjusted net expectancy and capital protection,
not daily profit quotas or maximum trade count. Zero-trade/zero-investment days are
valid. Never loosen filters, force fills, increase risk, or rewrite negative evidence
to manufacture daily earnings.

IMPLEMENTATION ORDER
[NEXT AFTER CURRENT FORWARD COLLECTION WINDOW]
1. Audit existing Investment History data/API/UI and Quality Dips V3 durable evidence.
2. Specify QUALITY_DIPS_PAPER_V1 entry, lot, sizing, mark, exit and terminal contracts.
3. Add append-only investment PAPER journal and restart-safe state.
4. Add deterministic PAPER fill/cost model and explicit skipped/rejected evidence.
5. Add focused lifecycle/idempotency/accounting tests.
6. Upgrade Investment History UI to Archive V2.
7. Add cross-strategy read-only scorecard without mixing incompatible metrics.
8. Run full regression and desktop runtime acceptance.
9. Collect untouched Quality Dips forward PAPER evidence; do not tune on the final
   holdout/forward window.
10. Only after mature positive evidence, open a separate live-readiness review.

STATUS:
[ROADMAP AUTHORIZED / IMPLEMENTATION NOT YET STARTED]
This roadmap update changes no runtime strategy, PAPER position, brokerage behavior,
threshold, or live-capital permission.

===============================================================================
2026-10-05 UNIVERSAL PAPER EXECUTION 3 — LOT ACCOUNTING + ARCHIVE API
===============================================================================
Status: [GREEN]

Implemented:
- QUALITY_DIPS_PAPER_V1 append-only mark_lot lifecycle.
- Explicit observed PAPER marks calculate market value, unrealized P&L and return.
- Closed lots reject later marks.
- Idempotent close_lot lifecycle with explicit observed exit price and reason.
- Duplicate close acknowledgement cannot rewrite the original exit.
- portfolio_snapshot rebuilds state entirely from durable journal events.
- Open-lot accounting includes latest mark, unrealized P&L/return, observed MFE/MAE.
- Per-symbol aggregation includes shares, weighted cost basis, cost basis,
  market value and unrealized P&L.
- Closed-lot accounting separates realized P&L from open unrealized P&L.

EXECUTION 23 — POST-SOAK INSPECTION + OPPORTUNITY TRACKER HEALTH TRUTH — GREEN
Date: 2026-10-05 CT
================================================================================

PURPOSE
- Harvest real post-market-session evidence before changing code.
- Investigate the Command Center "Opportunity Tracker — STOPPED" finding.
- Investigate Prediction PAPER "auto-flat blocked / deadline violation" evidence.
- Preserve all forward PAPER evidence and all live-capital prohibitions.

CONFIRMED FINDINGS
1. Opportunity Tracker health false-negative:
   - OpportunityTracker is started by app.main at runtime.
   - Worker state is stored in OpportunityTracker._running.
   - /health reads the public .running attribute.
   - The class did not expose .running, so getattr(..., False) always rendered STOPPED.
   - This was an observability defect, not evidence that the worker had actually stopped.

2. Prediction PAPER terminal evidence:
   - Historical PAPER trade: KXRAIN-26OCT03-PIT, YES, 10 contracts, entry $0.0200.
   - Required pre-event exit ultimately had NO_EXECUTABLE_DEPTH.
   - Atlas preserved the event as EXPIRED_UNCLOSED / EXIT_FAILED_NO_LIQUIDITY.
   - It did NOT fabricate an exit, closed trade, PnL, ROI, or win/loss.
   - Current Prediction eligibility already requires executable entry depth AND executable
     exit depth at entry, rejecting NO_EXECUTABLE_EXIT_DEPTH_AT_ENTRY or
     INSUFFICIENT_EXECUTABLE_EXIT_DEPTH_AT_ENTRY.
   - Therefore no speculative Prediction threshold change was made from this one terminal
     historical observation.
   - Unattended Prediction PAPER opening remains blocked pending validation.

3. Forward evidence observed:
   - Day Trading closed PAPER sample reached 1,145.
   - Aggregate native expectancy observed: -0.0956 R per closed trade.
   - Aggregate win rate observed: 0.414.
   - This is evidence, not an automatic strategy action. Future analysis must segment by
     strategy/version/regime before any conclusion or threshold change.
   - QUALITY_DIPS_PAPER_V1 remains 0 open / 0 closed; inactivity is not fabricated into
     evidence.
   - Prediction remains 0 valid closed sample, 0 open, 1 expired-unclosed terminal record.
   - Checkpoint exclusion accounting reconciled 8 physical rows = 8 categorized rows:
     5 usable, 3 replay/duplicate, all other exclusion buckets zero.
   - Excluded checkpoint rows remain excluded from evidence.

================================================================================
FUTURE EXPANSION — CONTROLLED INTERNET / ALPHA INTELLIGENCE LAYER
================================================================================

GOAL
Give Atlas controlled access to public internet intelligence so it can discover and preserve
potential alpha/catalyst context without allowing untrusted web content to directly execute
trades or silently mutate frozen strategies.

DESIGN REQUIREMENTS
- Dedicated intelligence/ingestion layer separated from strategy execution.
- Source classes should include official exchange/project announcements, reputable financial
  and crypto news, macro/economic releases, market/sector information, and carefully governed
  public/social intelligence where useful.
- Every intelligence item should retain source identity/URL, retrieval time, publication time
  when available, affected symbols/markets, freshness, confidence/reliability class, and the
  reason Atlas considered it relevant.
- Deduplicate repeated stories and distinguish primary/official sources from secondary reports,
  commentary, rumors, and social claims.
- Never treat internet text as trusted instructions to Atlas.
- Never allow web content to override safety locks, frozen thresholds, position sizing, or
  execution permissions.
- Initial use is decision-support/context only.
- Preserve what Atlas knew BEFORE an opportunity so later forward analysis can test whether the
  intelligence actually improved outcomes without hindsight leakage.
- Only evidence-proven intelligence features may later be considered for strategy integration,
  through a separate reviewed execution.

POSSIBLE FUTURE SURFACES
- Alpha / Catalyst feed in the Atlas frontend.
- Symbol-linked intelligence cards on Perps, Investment, and Prediction pages.
- Breaking-event relevance and freshness scoring.
- Official-source provenance and confidence badges.
- Pre-trade "what changed / why this matters" context.
- Forward outcome study comparing opportunities with/without specific intelligence features.

Phase D - surfaces:
- Alpha/Catalyst feed.

- Discord alerts for genuinely material corroborated events with dedup/cooldown.
Phase E - research:
- Measure whether intelligence features improve prospective PAPER selection/calibration.
- Internet evidence may become a versioned model feature only after controlled validation.
- It never bypasses risk locks, sizing limits, strategy freezes or execution permissions.

================================================================================
EXECUTION 31 - CONTROLLED INTERNET/ALPHA INTELLIGENCE FOUNDATION - GREEN
Date: 2026-10-05 CT
================================================================================
PURPOSE
- Establish the governed context-only foundation for future Internet/Alpha intelligence.
- Define normalized external intelligence events with explicit provenance, trust, freshness, rumor, corroboration and contradiction state.
- Treat all fetched/external text as untrusted data and provide no execution or strategy-mutation authority.
- Keep E29 forward Perps collection unchanged.

IMPLEMENTATION
- New backend/app/services/alpha_intelligence.py.
- New GET /api/validation/e31-alpha-contract.
- New tests/test_alpha_intelligence.py.
- No live fetchers or broad Internet ingestion were enabled in E31; this execution builds the contract that future ingestion must obey.

SOURCE REGISTRY CONTRACT
- SourceRecord requires source_id, name, source_class, trust_tier and hostname domain.
- Source classes: OFFICIAL, NEWS, MACRO, MARKET_STRUCTURE, SOCIAL, OTHER.
- Trust tiers: PRIMARY_OFFICIAL, REPUTABLE_REPORTING, MARKET_STRUCTURE, GOVERNED_SOCIAL, UNKNOWN.
- Event URL hostname must exactly match the registered source domain or normalization rejects the event.
- This prevents an event from claiming provenance from one source while pointing to another domain.

NORMALIZED INTELLIGENCE EVENT
- Schema version: alpha-intel-event-v1.
- Preserves source ID/class/trust, URL/domain, retrieval timestamp, publication timestamp, title/summary, event type, symbols/entities and content fingerprint.
- Event types include LISTING, DELISTING, PROTOCOL, REGULATORY, MACRO, SECURITY, FUNDING_OI, LIQUIDATION, PARTNERSHIP, TOKENOMICS and OTHER.
- Every event carries explicit flags:
  * untrusted_external_text=true
  * execution_authority=false
  * strategy_mutation_authority=false
  * membership_mutation_authority=false
  * threshold_mutation_authority=false

FRESHNESS / RUMOR / CORROBORATION
- Source-class-aware maximum ages are defined; stale state is explicit.
- Publication timestamp is preferred for age; retrieval timestamp is used when publication time is unavailable.
- GOVERNED_SOCIAL starts rumor_only=true unless already corroborated.
- Cross-source corroboration requires a separate domain with the same normalized claim fingerprint.
- Corroboration clears rumor-only state but does not grant execution authority.
- Content fingerprint represents event/claim content rather than source domain, while provenance remains separate.
- This design was specifically corrected after the first focused test exposed that domain-bearing fingerprints would block cross-source corroboration.

CONTRADICTION / DEDUP
- Related same-event-type/symbol claims with opposing simple polarity are flagged contradiction=true.
- Contradictory claims are preserved rather than silently rewritten into one answer.
- Deduplication uses normalized claim fingerprint and prefers higher-trust evidence while retaining the winning source's original provenance.
- E31 contradiction detection is deliberately a foundation primitive, not a claim of semantic completeness.

SAFETY CONTRACT
- external_text_is_untrusted_data=true
- execution_authority=false
- live_capital_allowed=false
- automatic_real_money_execution=false
- strategy_mutation_authority=false
- membership_mutation_authority=false
- threshold_mutation_authority=false
- paper_entry_authority=false
- Alpha context cannot place orders, open PAPER trades, retune E28/E29, or alter challenger membership.

VALIDATION
- First focused run: 22 passed, 2 failed. Failures correctly exposed a claim-fingerprint design bug: including domain prevented identical claims from separate domains from corroborating/deduplicating.
- Fixed before commit by separating claim fingerprint from source provenance.
- Final focused E31/E30/E29/E28/E27 suite: 24 passed, 0 failed.
- git diff --check GREEN.
- Full Atlas regression: 1,238 passed, 0 failed, 2 known dependency warnings in 129.25s.
- Known warnings remain Starlette/httpx TestClient deprecation and Discord audioop deprecation.
- E31 implementation commit: 60e6726.

RUNTIME ACCEPTANCE
- Controlled ATLAS.bat restart completed and Atlas reported running.
- API healthy.
- Live health: status=ok, Opportunity Tracker=true, Day Trade=true, Prediction=true, Discord=true, liquid_count=80.
- Live E31 contract: schema=alpha-intel-event-v1, external text untrusted=true, execution=false, membership mutation=false, threshold mutation=false, paper entry=false, live capital=false, automatic real-money execution=false.
- E29 prospective evidence remains unchanged: NOT_READY, baseline closes=0, challenger closes=0.

STATUS
- Execution 31 GREEN.

- No external news/social feed is yet driving strategy selection or execution.

EXECUTION 32 - GOVERNED ALPHA INGESTION STORE + READ-ONLY CATALYST FEED - GREEN
Date: 2026-10-05 CT
================================================================================
PURPOSE
- Convert the E31 context contract into an auditable ingestion/store pipeline.
- Use a small explicit governed source registry rather than open-ended crawling.
- Preserve raw external observations separately from normalized intelligence events.
- Expose a read-only Alpha/Catalyst feed with zero execution or strategy authority.
- Keep E29 forward Perps collection unchanged.

IMPLEMENTATION
- New backend/app/services/alpha_ingestion.py.
- New GET /api/validation/e32-alpha-store.
- New GET /api/validation/e32-alpha-feed.
- New tests/test_alpha_ingestion.py.
- Store version: alpha-ingestion-store-v1.
- No autonomous web crawler/fetch worker is started in E32.

GOVERNED SOURCE REGISTRY V1
- Hyperliquid official docs: OFFICIAL / PRIMARY_OFFICIAL / hyperliquid.gitbook.io.
- Coinbase official: OFFICIAL / PRIMARY_OFFICIAL / www.coinbase.com.
- U.S. SEC: OFFICIAL / PRIMARY_OFFICIAL / www.sec.gov.
- Federal Reserve: MACRO / PRIMARY_OFFICIAL / www.federalreserve.gov.
- Reuters: NEWS / REPUTABLE_REPORTING / www.reuters.com.
- Unknown source IDs are rejected before storage.
- URL hostname must match the governed source record through the E31 normalizer.

AUDITABLE TWO-LAYER STORAGE
- Raw append-only observations: backend/data/alpha_intelligence_raw.jsonl.
- Normalized append-only events: backend/data/alpha_intelligence_events.jsonl.
- Raw records preserve observed_at, source_id, complete payload, untrusted_external_text=true and execution_authority=false.
- Normalized records preserve E31 source/trust/provenance/freshness/event metadata.
- File writes flush and fsync before returning.
- Duplicate event_id is rejected without appending a second raw or normalized record.
- Storage is auditable even when the operator feed later deduplicates equivalent claims.

READ-ONLY ALPHA/CATALYST FEED
- Reconstructs normalized events from the append-only store.
- Applies E31 corroboration/contradiction annotation.
- Optional stale filtering.
- Deduplicates equivalent claims for operator presentation while retaining stored evidence.
- Prefers higher-trust source when equivalent claims collapse to one feed item.
- Returns source_count, stored_event_count, feed_count and normalized items.
- Feed and store status both carry the E31 zero-authority safety contract.

IMPORTANT PIPELINE FIX FOUND BY TESTS
- Initial focused run exposed an ordering flaw: a stale duplicate with a valid publication timestamp could win dedup before freshness filtering and suppress a fresher observation with malformed/missing publication time.
- Corrected before commit to annotate -> freshness filter -> deduplicate.
- Stale evidence remains in append-only storage but cannot suppress fresher usable context from a non-stale feed.

MALICIOUS / INSTRUCTION-LIKE TEXT
- Test stores text equivalent to instructions to ignore rules, place a live order, and change challenger thresholds/membership.
- Text remains preserved verbatim as untrusted external data.
- It receives no execution, PAPER-entry, threshold, membership or strategy mutation authority.
- No external text is interpreted as Atlas instructions.

EXECUTION 33 - BOUNDED GOVERNED ALPHA FETCH ADAPTERS - GREEN
Date: 2026-10-05 CT
================================================================================
PURPOSE
- Add the first bounded real public-source fetch adapters on top of E31/E32.
- Keep fetch success separate from intelligence-event truth.
- Enforce strict network/provenance/resource boundaries before broader Alpha ingestion.
- Perform a bounded live acceptance without manufacturing events.

IMPLEMENTATION
- New backend/app/services/alpha_fetch.py.
- New tests/test_alpha_fetch.py.
- No autonomous fetch worker was added to Atlas startup.
- No parser currently promotes fetched pages into normalized events.

FROZEN FETCH TARGETS
- Federal Reserve press releases: www.federalreserve.gov.
- U.S. SEC press releases: www.sec.gov.
- Hyperliquid official documentation: hyperliquid.gitbook.io.
- Fetch URL must exactly equal the frozen target for its source ID.
- Final redirect hostname must remain the governed source domain.
- Open-ended crawling is not permitted.

NETWORK BOUNDARIES
- Request timeout: 8 seconds.
- Maximum accepted response body: 512,000 bytes.
- Redirects followed only if final hostname remains governed.
- Allowed content types: text/html, application/xml, text/xml, application/rss+xml, application/atom+xml, application/json.
- User agent identifies Project Atlas research.
- Successful source cooldown: 300 seconds.
- Fetch failures/rejections are telemetry, not intelligence truth.

FETCH TELEMETRY
- Append-only telemetry path: backend/data/alpha_fetch_telemetry.jsonl.
- Records source ID, URL, finish time, outcome, HTTP status, bytes, content type, latency, parse result, rejection reason and execution_authority=false.
- Successful body is represented by SHA-256 in telemetry rather than being treated as an instruction.
- HTTP success currently records parse_result=NO_SUPPORTED_CLAIM.
- No claim parser means no event is created merely because a page was fetched successfully.

SECURITY / INERT CONTENT
- Fixture tests include instruction-like text requesting live orders and threshold changes.
- It remains fetched content only; no event or execution action is produced.
- Cross-domain redirects are rejected.
- Oversized responses and non-allowlisted content types are rejected.
- Cooldown prevents repeated network calls inside the governed interval.

VALIDATION

- Full Atlas regression: 1,255 passed, 0 failed, 2 known dependency warnings in 128.20s.
- Known warnings remain Starlette/httpx TestClient deprecation and Discord audioop deprecation.
- E33 implementation commit: e0618df.

================================================================================
EXECUTION 48 - BOUNDED TYPED CROSS-LANE DISCORD EVENT CONTRACT - GREEN - 2026-10-06
================================================================================

OBJECTIVE
- Establish a typed cross-lane Discord event envelope and routing contract without granting Discord any strategy or execution authority.
- Prove event identity, provenance sensitivity, explicit route eligibility, successful-send acknowledgement, retry-on-failure, dedup, and hostile external-text inertness.
- Keep migration of existing producers bounded and incremental rather than claiming a completed all-lane cutover.

IMPLEMENTATION
- Added backend/app/services/e48_discord_events.py as a pure downstream notification-policy layer beside the existing Discord DM transport.
- Frozen bounded route lanes: ALPHA_CATALYST, PREDICTION_ELIGIBLE, PERP_ALERT, SYSTEM.
- Each lane has an explicit allowlist of event types, destination, and cooldown policy metadata.
- Typed DiscordEvent contains deterministic event_id, lane, event_type, severity, title/body data, provenance, and materiality.
- Event identity is SHA-256-derived from typed identity/provenance inputs and is deterministic.
- Unknown lanes and unlisted event types fail closed.
- Non-material events are not delivery eligible.
- Delivery acknowledgement occurs only after sender success.
- Failed sends remain retryable and are not dedup-acknowledged.
- Successfully acknowledged events dedup and cannot flood the destination.
- External title/body text is forwarded only as display data and is never parsed as command/authority.
- Every routing/delivery decision explicitly carries false execution, PAPER-entry, strategy mutation, membership mutation, threshold mutation, promotion, and live-capital authority.
- Added tests/test_e48_discord_events.py.
- Existing legacy Discord producers were NOT silently rewired in E48; migration is intentionally deferred lane-by-lane to E49 under compatibility tests.

VALIDATION
- Focused E46-E48 + Prediction/perp Discord compatibility: 25 passed, 0 failed, 2 known dependency warnings in 2.73s.
- Hostile-text test used an external payload attempting to override rules and request execution/threshold mutation/PAPER opening; payload remained inert display data and all authority flags remained false.
- Duplicate flood test sent the same typed event ten times and confirmed one delivery plus nine dedups.
- Failure/retry/ack test confirmed failed delivery remains retryable, successful retry becomes acknowledged, and later duplicate does not send.
- git diff --check clean.
- Full Atlas regression: 1,318 passed, 0 failed, 2 known dependency warnings in 138.00s.
- Known warnings unchanged: discord audioop deprecation; Starlette/httpx TestClient deprecation.

AUXILIARY CONTRACT CLASSIFICATION
- Stock accumulation ladder: Redis keys atlas:accum:<symbol>:<level>:<kind>; successful Discord send precedes durable _mark; HIT additionally marks NEAR. Migration must preserve send-success -> mark ordering and one-alert-per-level semantics.
- BTC accumulation ladder: Redis keys atlas:btc:lvl:<level>:<kind>; successful send precedes durable _mark; HIT additionally marks NEAR; special legacy 63,500 level is marked without notification. Migration must preserve this ordering and special suppression.
- Quality Dips V3 delivery: durable event store delivery.delivered gates retries; mark_delivery(key, delivered=ok) and store.save() occur after sender result. Migration must preserve retry-safe durable event semantics exactly.
- Auto ladder: Redis NX created key and ladder definition are persisted BEFORE the informational Discord notification; notification failure must never roll back or duplicate ladder creation. Migration must preserve creation-before-notify ordering and protected-core exclusions.
- No contract above was changed by E54.
